GitHub connector (worker 2 lost T-091 twice, 01:25 and 07:43). Closed rows (MERGED, DONE, CLOSED, FOLDED, CANCELLED,
REJECTED with a rework row, PARKED) and board notes older than 2026-10-20 live here, unchanged. The dashboard builder reads
this file as well, so no task disappears from the owner's dashboard. Only the Lead and the verifier move rows here.
